# Cua Driver 0.34.0: third-party crate notices

Ahel Desktop ships the unmodified `cua-driver` executable from the trycua/cua release
`cua-driver-rs-v0.34.0` (MIT, Copyright (c) 2025 Cua AI, Inc.). This file lists every Rust crate
linked into that executable for macOS (arm64, x86_64) and Windows (x86_64), with the licence each
crate declares and the licence texts the crates ship.

Generated on 2026-10-06 from the tag source with
`cargo tree --locked -p cua-driver --target <triple> -e normal,build --format "{p}|{l}"`
and the licence files inside each crate archive from crates.io.

MPL-2.0 crates (`uniffi*` 0.31.0) are used unmodified; their source is available at
https://crates.io/crates/uniffi/0.31.0 and https://github.com/mozilla/uniffi-rs/tree/v0.31.0.
`BSL-1.0` below is the Boost Software License 1.0, a permissive licence.
